# Computer Organization and Architecture: question papers in full

127 unique papers · 2007-08 to 2025-26 · Computer Engineering, Information Technology, Computer Applications (MCA), Computer Science and Business Systems, Cyber Security, AI and Machine Learning, Data Science, Common (all branches), Electronics

Each paper: year, exam, semester, departments and programmes, then its full text as extracted. Scanned papers were read by OCR, so equations and symbols may contain recognition errors. Structured data: [`questions.jsonl`](questions.jsonl) (one record per question) and [`papers.jsonl`](papers.jsonl) (one record per paper). Index of files: [`README.md`](README.md).

We learned three different addressing modes for referring to
operands in MIPS assembly. They are register mode, immediate
mode, and base-offset mode. Since a given instruction can have more
than one operand, it can use multiple addressing modes. For the
below instructions, list all the specific addressing modes that the
instruction uses. (Instructions: add, beq, lw)

not change during the program's execution (run time).

the role of this multiplexer.

Name different types of pipeline hazards.

one is typically more?

Name the three types of cache misses?

Define cache miss rate for a specific application.
